#### Change Contract Classification

Added 2026-11-14 under SPEC-0213 (W03-D77, PKG W00:822-831). One row for every open row of `Residue Handoff` as read before this package's closure and cell edits (76 rows, equal to the 76 open ledger entries), for the six seeds that this package adds, and for each other package in the tree. The class names are analysis labels only (W00:824); none is a registry state or a lifecycle state. Only `CANCEL_OBSOLETE` is defined by the PKG (W00:828: a requirement the user removed, never a PASS); the other four are this Spec's operational reading for residue rows: `KEEP_VALID` the obligation is valid and its owner is right, `REVISE` the valid part is kept and a cell is corrected, `SUPERSEDE` a new owner replaces it, `REMOVE_AFTER_TRANSFER` the obligation is closed or transferred and its entry leaves the ledger. In the PKG (W00:827) `REMOVE_AFTER_TRANSFER` means removing an old package after its obligations are transferred; applied to a ledger row it is a stretch of that meaning. One owner rule holds for every row (W00:831): the Owner cell names exactly one stage; another stage a cell names moves to the Resume condition as a consumer. "W00" as an owner means the SPEC-0161 Task's W00 intake record, the workspace's existing Task, not a W00 stage run. The Current owner column shows the owner after the corrections of this package. Result: `REMOVE_AFTER_TRANSFER` 1 (W01-H01); `REVISE` 16 (13 edited here, W00-H01, W03-H02, GOV-04, R147-03, OPEN-01, OPEN-02, OPEN-03, OPEN-04, OPEN-06, W03-H15, W03-H29, SDLC-07 and R45-12, and 3 edited later by SPEC-0211, OPEN-07, DOC-12 and DOC-S99); `KEEP_VALID` 66 (59 open rows, 6 seeds and SPEC-0213); `SUPERSEDE` 0 and `CANCEL_OBSOLETE` 0, so their absence is a result. A `KEEP_VALID` row without a named PKG line is decided by the live row, its recorded decision and W00:831. The eight terminal rows (LEDGER, W03-H08, W03-H16, W03-H27, W03-H30, W03-H33, W03-H36 and W03-H42) are not live obligations and are not classified. The first column is a running number, not a residue ID. The W00 completion test (W00:831: every live obligation has exactly one owner; no deleted package keeps an active link, check input or Task reference) is shown only after the last W03 package: this package claims only the rows it classifies, OPEN-07 is shown to have one owner only after SPEC-0211, and SPEC-0211's closing report reads and re-classifies this table.

Nothing is filled in here, nothing is inferred from the third column, and no work residue ledger entry exists for these facts (W03-D67 Q3). It stays until the W00 exit.

| Field | What a verifying record states | Exists today (not proof) | Supplied by |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `approved_by` | The person or role that approved `WGOV-CORE` 3.0.0 for use, in a record the reference points to: the decision text or transcript behind `approval_ref` `call_415633a444f34091860dcb4b8700431d/0`, a review approval in the source repository `Project-Template` at source revision `bc5b70556c57198768119f94c287f93c87964482`, or a direct statement of the request owner recorded as a Task user answer. | The opaque reference. The `docs/99.templates/README.md` paragraph that begins `Registry의 shared_contract는` says in prose that buenhyden approved the exact original, that the source bundle is 67 repository-relative paths hashed by a stated digest rule, and that the language and first-record extensions are not a new edition or a full local adoption; a README sentence is not an approval record. `owner` `buenhyden` is a local declaration. | The user |
| `approved_at` | The date on the same record. | None. | The user |
| `effective_from` | The date or commit from which this repository follows 3.0.0, stated by the approver or the adopting Task. | The registry stage first reads `adopted` in commit `92849f15` (2026-10-16), whose evidence is the SPEC-0115 Task 0005 (recoverable through the REQ-0003 Archived Lineage); the stage was still `candidate` at `2a99a8bb` (2026-10-09). These show when the registry began to say `adopted`, not when the adoption took effect. | The user, who may confirm that commit |
| `scope` | What the approval covers (the whole contract or listed parts) and its exceptions. | The `extensions` list shows what this repository adds; the README sentences above state the 67-path bundle and that the extensions are not a new edition. Neither states what was approved. | The user |
| `migration` | How this repository moved to 3.0.0 from its earlier edition, or a statement that none was needed. | The SPEC-0115 Task 0005 evidence at `92849f15`, which the author reads and quotes only if it states a migration. | The user, or the author from that Task |
| `rollback` | How the adoption is undone and who decides. | None stated as a decision; reverting the adoption commit is a possibility, not a record. | The user |

#### 3.0.1 Verdicts

Added 2026-11-15 under SPEC-0216 (EVD-132). These rows are analysis for the stage owners. They are not residue rows, not a progress record, and they accept nothing. The first column is a running number, never a residue ID. Each row has one owner stage. Carried by names the live row or the PKG prompt through which that stage takes the row in at its own intake. No ledger entry is added. `REPLACE` and `REMOVE` have no row. A row read only through PKG's observation is `UNVERIFIED`. No row lowers a requirement to match the tree. Historical audit questions (OPEN-01 to OPEN-04, OPEN-06 and W00-H01) are not reactivated.

Design summary (PKG W00-DELTA). Designs D00, D15 and D17, edition 3.0.1, with external items S03 to S07, S10, S20 to S22, S24 and S27 read for their applicability and limits. Choice: W00 records verdicts and owners only; each `CHANGE` is fixed by its owning stage under the user's requirements. Rejected alternatives: seeding ledger rows, which duplicates the stage prompts that already carry each item; fixing the documents here, which is outside W00's write boundary; and re-running W03. Interface: these tables, which the stage owners read at their own intake. Failure: a PKG hash that differs from `manifest.json` stops the intake. Risk: a verdict being read as acceptance; each row therefore states its read scope. Acceptance test: SPEC-0216 VAL-W00D-001. The two input archives of `input-provenance.json` are absent, so their own analysis is not repeated; PKG's derived files are used instead.

#### W01 3.0.1 Rule Fit

Added 2026-11-15 under SPEC-0217 (EVD-133). This is the W01-CONTRACT rule-fit record against PKG 3.0.1 and its confirmed decisions. It is analysis, not a residue table and not a progress record. The first column is a running number, never a residue ID. Each row names one owning stage, which takes the row in at its own intake under its own PKG prompt; no ledger entry is added.

How each rule's purpose, owner, consumer and enforcement are explained: purpose by the rule's body text; owner by its policy file; consumer and enforcement by the Evidence column of its Rules table. A named check is a mechanical control, and `Semantic review` is not mechanically enforced. The 218 rules of the ten governance policies all carry these, and this table judges their fit only.

Limits: verdicts are text-level. Three read-only subagents read every rule, this session spot-read the duplicates and the overstated findings, and the evidence checks named in the Rules tables were not run. GIT-R012, GIT-R013, FMT-R002, FMT-R003 and FMT-R010 were found to be links or permitted mirrors and are `KEEP_VERIFIED`. GIT-R011 (one approval per commit or push) is kept, because Q08 removes repeated approval of edits, not of outward delivery.
